‘kaṭaitiṟantēṉ koḷvōr illai’ eṉṟa vaḷḷalāriṉ karuttuppaṭi nāḷitaḻ veḷivaruvatu niṉṟu viṭṭatu. oru nāḷ avar kaṇkalaṅkic colliya coṟkaḷ ivai: “kalappaṭattamiḻilum, koccait tamiḻilum veḷivarukiṟa itaḻkaḷilum, kavarccip paṭaṅkaḷōṭum niḻaṟpaṭac ceytikaḷōṭum veḷivarukiṟa tamiḻ itaḻkaḷilum nāṭṭam koḷḷukiṉṟa nam makkaḷiṉ maṉam, nalla tamiḻil veḷivarukiṉṟa nāḷitaḻkaḷil collavillaiyē! itaṟku eṉṉa ceyvatu? tamiḻum tamiḻakamum etirkālattil eṉṉavākum” eṉpatē.

pulavar kuḻu

irupattināṉku āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu tirucciyil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa kaṭaiccaṅka kālattiya pulavarkaḷaip pōṉṟa nāṟpattoṉpatu pulavar perumakkaḷaṭaṅkiya tamiḻakap pulavarkaḷ kuḻuvai maturaikku aḻaittu, taṉ illattil viruntaḷittu, aṉaivarukkum puttāṭai koṭuttu maturait tamiḻc caṅkattil vaittut tamiḻ ārāyac ceytu perumakiḻcci aṭaintavar avar.

kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷ

evariṭattum naṉkoṭai peṟāmal taṉ varuvāyaik koṇṭē kalaikkallūri, poṟiyiyaṟ kallūri, āciriyar payiṟcik kallūri, cila uyarnilaippaḷḷikaḷ, pala toṭakkap paḷḷikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟait toṭaṅkip potumakkaḷukku utaviya peruntakaiyāḷar karumuttu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ oru aracāṅkam ceyyavēṇṭiya paṇikaḷait tiyākarācar taṉi oruvarāka ceytu muṭittirukkiṟār eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum.

kalaiyaḻaku

kalaittantai avarkaḷ oru kaṭṭaṭak kalaiñar. kaṭṭaṭattilum oru kalaiyaḻakai, kalaiyaḻakilum oru taṉit taṉmaiyaik kaṇṭavar. ceṉṉai maturai, koṭaikkāṉal, kuṟṟālam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa avaratu māḷikaikaḷil avariṉ kaivaṇṇattai, kalaiyaḻakiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyaik kaṇṭu makiḻalām.

araciyal

1938–kkup piṟaku kāṅkiracil iruntu vilakiṉār. inti etirppuk kaṭcikkup piṟaku enta araciyal kaṭciyilum avar talaiyiṭavillai. naṭu nilaimai vakittut tamiḻppaṇi maṭṭum purintuvantār. eṉṟālum pala araciyal talaivarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpuk koṇṭiruntār. avarkaḷil car.pi.ṭi. irājaṉ, W.F.A. ceḷantara pāṇṭiyaṉār, periyār ī.vē.rā., viññāṉamētai G.D.nāyuṭu, car A. irāmacāmi mutaliyār, A. T. paṉṉīr celvam, rājāji, 0.P.R, kāmarāj mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭat takuntavarkaḷ. tērtal

oru cāyam avar, avaratu makaṉkaḷ cuntaram, māṇikkavācakam ākiya mūvarum orē nērattil pala ūrkaḷil pala tokutikaḷil caṭṭamaṉṟat tērtalukku niṉṟārkaḷ. karumuttu avarkaḷiṉ tokutiyil avarōṭu nāṉ maṭṭumē pēcavēṇṭum eṉpatu avaruṭaiya tiṭṭamum koḷkaiyum ākum. ataṉpaṭi nāṉ avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu, tērtal kūṭṭaṅkaḷil maturai, tirumaṅkalam, cōḻavantāṉ, civakaṅkai, kāraikkuṭi, mēluār mutaliya pala ūrkaḷil pēciṉēṉ. avaratu aruntoṇṭukaḷaiyum, peruñceyalkaḷaiyum eṭuttu viḷakkiṉēṉ. pēcip palaṉ eṉṉa? payaṉ oṉṟumillai. mūvaril oruvarkūṭa veṟṟi peṟavillai. itu tērtalil nallavarkaḷ niṟkum cūḻnilai iṉṉum uruvākavillai eṉpataiyē kāṭṭiṟṟu.

camayappaṟṟu

caivacamayattil āḻnta paṟṟu uṭaiyavar. tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭṭil ciṟantavar. caiva uṇavaiyē uṇpavar. avaratu mukattait tirunīṟu eppōtum aḻaku ceytu koṇṭirukkum.

viruntu

karumuttu māḷikaiyil viruntukaḷ palarukku naṭai peṟṟa vaṇṇamirukkum. avviruntil cuvaiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ palavirukkum. avaṟṟiṉ cuvaiyaiviṭa avaratu maṉaiviyār irātā ammaiyāratu iṉcoṟkaṉiṉ cuvai mikaiyāka irukkum. avarkaḷ iruvarum kaṭaiciyāka aḷitta viruntu eṉakkum eṉ maṉaivikkumē. atu avaratu cāviṉ mutal nāḷ kuṟṟālattil irutta poḻutu.

maṟaivu

maṟunāṭ kālai 6 maṇikkut taṉ makaṉ kaṇṇaṉai maturaikku aṉuppiviṭṭu, maṉaiviyaiyum aruvikku aṉuppiviṭṭu, tāṉ kuḷippataṟkāka eṇṇeyaiyum tuṇṭaiyum eṭuttuvara vēlaiyāḷaiyum aṉuppiviṭṭu, aruviyai nōkkit tiruvācakattaip pāṭiyapaṭi naṭantu koṇṭiruntār. aimpataṭi tūram naṭantatum kaiyai ūṉṟi uṭkārntār. talai cāyntatu. uṭaṉē ellōrum cērntu avarkaḷai oru tuppaṭṭiyil kiṭattit tūkki vantu paṭukkaiyil kiṭatti maruttuvarukkum ceyti aṉuppi, maruttuvarum uṭaṉē vantucērntār. ivai aṉaittum ceytu muṭiya ēḻu nimiṭaṅkaḷē piṭittaṉa. maruttuvar avaraip paricōtittu uyir pirintu ēḻu nimiṭaṅkaḷ āyiṉa. eṉak kūṟiviṭṭār. ellōrum kataṟi aḻutōm. avaratu poṉṉuṭalukku mutal mālaiyiṭum nilai eṉakku nēriṭṭatu. pala iṭaṅkaḷukku tolaipēcic ceytikaḷ paṟantaṉa. makaṉ kaṇṇaṉaiyum irājapāḷaiyattiṟkuc ceyti aṉuppi vaḻimaṟittut tiruppi aḻaittuk koṇṭōm. kārkaḷ palavantaṉa. avaratu poṉṉuṭaluṭaṉ nāṉum, rātā ammaiyāruṭaṉ eṉ maṉaiviyumāka aḻutu koṇṭē maturai vantu cērntōm. mīṉāṭci ālaiyiṉ uḷḷum puṟamum, avaratu illattiṉ uḷḷēyum veḷiyilum, vītiyiṉ vaḻineṭukilum makkaḷ kūṭṭam kaṭal alaikaḷ pōṉṟu peruki niṉṟu kataṟi aḻutu koṇṭirunta kāṭci eṅkaḷ tuṉpattai mēlum vaḷaracceytatu. poṉṉuṭalaik kāṇavanta makkaḷ kūṭṭam vaikaiyil kūṭum aḻakar tiruviḻāk kūṭṭattaiyum miñciyiruntatu.

niṉaivuc ciṉṉam

avaratu uṭal erikkappaṭuvatai nāṉ virumpavillai. avaratu māḷikaiyiṉ piṉ puṟattilēyē aṭakkam ceytu, niṉaivu ciṉṉam oṉṟu aṅku eḻuppa vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. ataṟkākap peritum muyaṉṟēṉ. tōlviyaiyē aṭaintēṉ. iṟutiyil avaratu poṉmēṉi erikkappaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu.

eṉṟu kāṇpōm

karumuttuvai, kalaittantaiyai, toḻilatiparai, peruñcelvarai, koṭaivaḷḷalai, tamiḻaṟiñarai, ēḻai paṅkāḷarai, eḷiya vāḻviṉarai, eḷiya uṭaiyiṉarai, iṉiya colliṉarai, avaratu iṉmukattai, puṉcirippai, nām iṉi eṉṟum eṅkum kāṇappōvatillai. avaratu iḻappu tamiḻukku, tamiḻarkku, tamiḻakattiṟku pēriḻappāka muṭintatu. yār? yārukku āṟutal kūṟuvatu?

mella nakarntu cellum kālamtāṉ nam aṉaivarkkum nallāṟutal kūṟavēṇṭum.

⁠vāḻka karumuttuviṉ pukaḻ!

﻿vaḷarka avar ceyta paṇikaḷ!





﻿﻿﻿toḻilatipar vi. cēṣacāyi toḻilaṟiñar



tiruccirāppaḷḷi pala tamiḻaṟiñarkaṉaiyum, icaip pulavarkaḷaiyum, uyarnta naṭikarkaḷaiyum, ciṟanta vaṇikarkaḷaiyum peṟṟu utaviyatu maṭṭumalla, atu nalla toḻilaṟiñarkaḷaiyum peṟṟuttantu perumaiyaṭaintirukkiṟatu. attakaiya talaiciṟanta toḻilaṟiñarkaḷil tiru. vi. cēṣacāyi avarkaḷum oruvar.

perumakaṉ

tamiḻakattiṉ perunakaraṅkaḷil oṉṟākiya tirucciyiṉ kaṭum iruḷaippōkka miṉ viḷakkai ēṟṟivaittu oḷitanta perumakaṉ. immuyaṟciyil kolli malaiyiliruntu miṉcāram koṇṭuvara mēlaināṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ muyaṉṟu tōlvi aṭaintataik kaṇṭum, perum muyaṟci ceytu veṟṟikaṇṭa pēraṟiñar avar.

ālai atipar

tirucci māvaṭṭam maṭṭumallāmal, piṟa māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum viññāṉat tuṟaiyil ālaikaḷai amaittu palvēṟu poruḷkaḷai uṇṭu paṇṇik koṭuttu innāṭṭiṉ tēvaikaḷai nirappivanta ālai atipar avar. ēḻai paṅkāḷar

tamatu nuṇṇaṟivāl, aruntiṟaṉāl, ēṟakkuṟaiya 7000 pērkaḷukku uḻaikka vaḻivakuttu avarkaḷiṉ uḻaippiṉ mūlam ēṟakkuṟaiya irupatāyiram pērkaḷ paciyiṉṟi vāḻa vakaiceytu vāḻntu maṟainta ēḻai paṅkāḷar avar.

tamiḻp pulavar

mikak kuṟainta nāṭkaḷil eṉṉiṭam, tirukkuṟaḷaip paṭittu ārāyat toṭaṅki, mika viraivil kuṟaḷ muḻuvataṟkum poruḷ kūṟum aṟivaiyum āṟṟalaiyum peṟṟuviṭṭa oru ciṟanta tamiḻppulavar avar.

cāstirikaḷ

vālmīki irāmāyaṇattai eḻutteṇṇip paṭittatōṭu, kamparāmāyaṇattaiyum naṉku kaṟṟu, iraṇṭiṟkum uḷḷa oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaikaḷaiyum kaṟpaṉai nayaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟumpoḻutu oru ciṟanta cāstiriyākak kāṭciyaḷittavar avar.

eḷiya tōṟṟam

avaratu āṭamparamaṟṟa eḷiya tōṟṟamum, aṉpukalanta iṉcollum naṇparai maṭṭumalla, pakaivaraiyum paṇiyac ceyyum valimai vāyntavai.

uyarnta paṇpu

piṟaruṭaiya tēvaiyai uṇaramaṟuppatum, piṟaruṭaiya urimaiyai oppamaṟuppatum ākiya iraṇṭum maṭṭumē nāṭṭil naṭaipeṟum pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷukkuk kāraṇam eṉpatai naṉkuṇarntu, ataṟkēṟṟapaṭi naṭantu kāṭṭiya uyarnta paṇpāḷar avar. peruṅkuṇam

ēḻai makkaḷukku irakkam kāṭṭutal, māṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷukku matippuk koṭuttal, piṭivātakkārarkaḷukku viṭṭuk koṭuttal, cakipput taṉmai, tārāḷa maṉappāṉmai ākiya aimperuṅ kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum oruṅkē peṟṟirunta kuṇakkuṉṟu avar.

nallaṟiñar

kuṭumpattilum toḻililum paṅku peṟṟu, vāḻvilum tāḻvilum uṟṟa tuṇaiyāki, eppōtum uṭaṉiruntu uḻaittu maṟainta maittuṉar tiru. ār. cēṣacāyi avarkaḷai uḷḷattē vaittu vāḻnta naṉṟi maṟavā nallaṟiñar avar.

cīrtiruttavāti

tāṉ tōṟṟiya teṉceṉṉai miṉvaḻaṅkum niṟuvaṉattiṟru eṉṉait talaivaṉākki, atai naṭattum poṟuppai eṉṉiṭam oppuvittu makiḻntu, cāti vēṟṟumai, camaya vēṟṟumai, nāṭṭu vēṟṟumai, moḻi vēṟṟumai, kaṭci vēṟṟumai, niṟa vēṟṟumai, uṇavu vēṟṟumai ākiya aṉaittaiyum pārātu, ellōraiyum tammavarāyk koṇṭu naṭantu kāṭṭiya ōr uyarnta cīrtiruttavāti avar.

pēccuk kalaiñar

atikārikaḷōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? aṉparkaḷōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? nirvākikaḷōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? toḻilāḷikaḷōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? maṉaiviyōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? makkaḷōṭu pēcuvatu eppaṭi? eṉpatai naṉkuṇarntu, arumaiyākap pēcip piṟaraiyum tammaip piṉpaṟṟip pēcumpaṭi ceyta pēccuk kalaiñar avar. koṭai vaḷḷal

caliyāmal muyaṟcittu, ōyāmal uḻaittu, nērmaiyāka naṭantu, nallavaḻiyil poruḷait tēṭi, cikkaṉamākac celaviṭṭu, eñciya poruḷai nalla aṟa nilaiyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku vāri vaḻaṅki makiḻnta koṭaivaḷḷal avar.

pēriḻappu



ittakaiya uyarnta toḻil nipuṇarum, ciṟanta moḻi aṟiñarum, niṟainta paṇpuṭaiyavarum, kaṉinta colluṭaiyavarum ākiya cāṉṟōr oruvarai iḻantatu, potuvākat tamiḻakattiṟkum, kuṟippākat tirucci nakarukkum oru pēriḻappu ākum.

pukaḻuṭampu



avaratu poyyuṭal maṟaintālum pukaḻuṭal maṟaivataṟkillai. avar koṭai vaḻaṅkivanta pala aṟa nilaiyaṅkaḷum, toṭaṅkivainta pala toḻil nilaiyaṅkaḷum, ariyamaṅkalattil tōṟṟivaitta toḻil nuṇukkap paḷḷiyum nilaittu niṟkumvarai, mēṭṭur kemikkals uḷḷavarai, īrōṭu kākita ālai uḷḷavarai, avaratu pukaḻuṭampu aḻiyātu. eṉiṉum, avaratu peyarāl oru poṟiiyaṟ kallūriyaiyum tōṟṟivaikka vēṇṭiyatu tamiḻaka makkaḷiṉ nīṅkāk kaṭamaikaḷil oṉṟākum.

āṟutal

“ippaṭippaṭṭa oruvar tirucciyil iruppatai nāṉ ivvaḷavu nāḷ aṟiyāmaṟ pōṉēṉē” eṉṟu avar eṉṉaippaṟṟi piṟariṭam kūṟiya coṟkaḷ eṉ kātil viḻuntapoḻututāṉ, “ippaṭippaṭṭa oruvar tirucciyil iruppatai nāṉ ivvaḷavu nāḷ aṟiyāmaṟ pōṉēṉē” eṉṟu nāṉum eṇṇiṉēṉ. eṉatu kaṇkaḷ iṉpak kaṇṇīrai tuḷirttaṉa. aṭuttu mika viraivil nērnta avaratu pirivu tuṉpak kaṇṇīrai utirkkumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭatu. nāṭu kalaṅkiṟṟu. nāmum kalaṅkiṉōm. yār yārukku āṟutal kūṟuvatu? mella nakarntu cellum kālamtāṉ nam aṉaivarkkum nal āṟutal aḷikkavēṇṭum.





﻿ ﻿﻿﻿tamiḻ maruntukaḷ

ki. ā. pe. vicuvanātam



“āṅkila āṭci vantatum citta āyurvēta yūṉāṇi mutaliya nam nāṭṭup paramparai vaittiyaṅkaḷ aḻintoḻintaṉa. nam nāṭṭup pāṭṭiyiṉ vaittiyamum pārttup pārttu ōṭiviṭṭatu. eṭuttataṟkellām ūci pōṭa ṭākṭarait tēṭum nilaimaiyum vanteytiyatu. ataṉāl, uṭampu maṭṭumiṉṟi uyirum pāḻākiyatu. ellām pāḻākiya piṉṉarē, tamiḻ makkaḷ taṅkaḷ paḻaiya celvaṅkaḷait tēṭa muṟpaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. nāṭu viṭutalaipeṟṟatum immuyaṟci mikavum ūkkattuṭaṉ naṭakkiṟatu. itaṟku utavi aḷikkum vakaiyilum, paḻaiya kaimmuṟai vaittiyaṅkaḷ aḻiyātu, pātukākkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṉum avā mikutiyiṉālum aṟiñar vicuvanātam avarkaḷ tam vīṭṭuk kaimmuṟaikaḷil palavaṟṟai innūluḷ tantuḷḷār. ittakaiya nūlkaḷ iṉṉum veḷivara vēṇṭum. mūla nōykku oṉpatu cikiccaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. innūlil oruvar kaiyil iruppatē oru vaittiyar uṭaṉ iruppataṟku nikar eṉṟu collalām. aṟiñar vicuvanātam avarkaḷ iṉṉum pala kaimmuṟaikaḷai aritiṉ muyaṉṟu peṟṟu veḷiyiṭuvārēl, tamiḻ makkaḷukkup pērutavi ceytavarāvar!’

‘tiṉakaraṉ’ matippurai



15–11–53

pāri nilaiyam,

184, pirāṭvē, ceṉṉai–108.

﻿﻿﻿﻿﻿aintu celvaṅkaḷ

ki. ā. pe. vicuvanātam

The five different kinds of wealth, which the author discusses in this book are Mothor, Imagination, Old Age Health and Character. In the first essay he expatiaies the greatness of the mother who also expresses herself in the mother country and the mother tongue and drives home to the Reader on the necessity of paying his homage to everyone of them. In the second essay the learned author leads the student to imagine and find out for himself the several uses of the eye and the ear and by series of questions and answers arrives at the conclusion that everyone should see little, speak Hess, but hear more. In the next chapter entitled ‘Weakth of Old age’ the value of experience is mentioned and incidently, the virtue of truthfulness emphasised; it would have been better if the teaching intended to be brought forth by the chapter heading. is more boldly brought forth. The next chapter deals with the nesessity of Physical exercises and the last chapter is a story of two boys who after a long search for true Wealth come to the conclusion that only good character and good behaviour form true Wealth. The style of the book is simple and direct.

—The “HINDU”



19-4-'53





pāri nilaiyam

184, pirāṭvē, ceṉṉai-108

inta miṉṉūlaip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇaiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭaittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇaiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷai varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇaintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷai aṉaivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻai ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭaippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uraiyai nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām
